### 📌 [Free – International Observe the Moon Night | 19 Sep 2026](https://seqas.org/event-6687519)
📆 Sat 19 Sep, 5:00 PM  
📍 Barrett Street Park, Bracken Ridge, Barrett Street Park, Bracken Ridge — enter via Jude Street, free parking available  
💰 Free  
`science` `nature` `community` `kids` `family` `beginners`

SEQAS is proud to host International Observe the Moon Night, NASA's annual worldwide celebration of Earth's Moon. Each year, observers around the globe come together on the same night to share direct observations of our nearest neighbour, and this year you can join them right here in Brisbane's north. Urban Observers is SEQAS's free monthly community telescope night at Barrett Street Park, Bracken Ridge. Our members will have their telescopes set up and pointed at the Moon, along with planets, star clusters and nebulae. Beginners and families are very welcome, and you don't need any equipment of your own. Just turn up, look through the eyepiece, and ask us anything. Register your attendance with us and we'll provide you with an official International Observe the Moon Night participation certificate. Free for All ages. Bring the kids!

### 🎵 [PETER MORÉN (Peter Bjorn And John, SunYears, Robert Forster’s band etc) intimate solo show! plus THE STIRLING CANON](https://thecaveinn.net/events/peter-moren-peter-bjorn-and-john-sunyears-robert-forsters-band-etc-intimate-solo-show-plus-the-stirling-canon/)
📆 Thu 17 Sep, 5:00 AM  
📍 The Cave Inn, 47 Balaclava St., Woolloongabba, QLD, 4102  
💰 AUD 0  
`indie` `folk` `acoustic` `live music`

PETER MORÉN (Peter Bjorn And John, SunYears, Robert Forsters band etc) intimate solosshow! Peter Morén is the lead singer and guitarist of Swedish indie-icons Peter Bjorn And John. He has previously toured Australia with them and played among other things Laneways Festival and Splendor in the Grass. He's now coming back down under to be guitarist/backing vocalist in Robert Forsters Swedish band (Peter produced his latest album "Strawberries" in Stockholm) and finds the time to do some intimate lowkey solo dates while here. Peter has recently primarily worked with his two lauded album as SunYears (2023s "Come Fetch My Soul!" and 2025s "The Song Forlorn"), sitting somewhere in between solowork/band. SunYears is driven by Peter as a new angle and fresh start in personal songwriting after a long life spent in music. With a variety of sonics ranging from wistful folk, Beatles-esque harmonies, psychedelic guitar jangle to shimmering balladry the melodic wisdom of SunYears on record has brought in guestvocals from Ron Sexmith, Fruit Bats, Madison Cunningham, Lisa Hanningan, Nicole Atkins and more. SunYears recently played at the heralded Kilkenny Roots Festival, Ireland. Peter Bjorn And John is currently touring behind the 20th anniversary of their classic album "Writers Block" with dates in USA/Canada, Mexico and Europe. Peter also recently released a collaborative single with Josh Rouse and played guitar on James Yorkstons three latest albums. Live with just a guitar Peter is free to skip between SunYears material, covers of favorites and maybe the occasional Peter Bjorn And John-classic. No setlist and no boundaries! Opportunity knocks for a unique and spontaneous evening! Don't miss out! Peter will be supported by The Stirling Canon, young and keen and tremendous. [button link="https://events.humanitix.com/peter-moren-and-the-stirling-canon"]Tickets[/button]

### 🎓 [ART TALKS – Beyond The Gallery: Art, Community and Connection with Katherine Kemp and Colin Bushell](https://rqas.com.au/event/art-talks-september-2026/)
📆 Sun 20 Sep, 2:00 PM  
📍 Petrie Terrace Gallery, 3/162 Petrie Terrace  
💰 See link  
`art` `community`

[vc_row css=”.vc_custom_1523319263370{padding-top: 20px !important;padding-bottom: 30px !important;}”][vc_column][vc_column_text] For our next ‘ART TALKS’ event, the Royal Queensland Art Society Brisbane Branch invites its members and the art loving community to: “Beyond The Gallery: Art, Community and Connection” In this presentation and Q&A, Katherine Kemp and Colin Bushell, co-founders of Art & Eats, will examine how they have drawn upon local energy to break down community barriers and build a creative ecosystem, not just an exhibition. They share their successes, the pitfalls and future vision, providing advice on building a model others can follow. Art & Eats delivers the principle of showcasing creativity, whilst bringing together community beyond the gallery. They believe in partnership before ownership and purpose before profit. Katherine is the owner of Ruby Red Jewellery and co-founder of Art & Eats. She is also a Jewellery designer, small-business owner, entrepreneur, valuer, gemologist and MBA recipient. Colin runs Colin Bushell Photography and is co-founder of Art & Eats with Katherine. He is also a multi-award-winning photographer, writer and photojournalist; co-founder and former President of The Gap Creative; former Vice President, Professional Photographers’ Association of Queensland. This talk will be held at RQAS Brisbane Gallery on Sunday, 20th of September from 2pm to 4pm . Afternoon tea is included in the ticket price. Ticket Prices: – $5 for RQAS Brisbane Members – $15 for the Public Don’t miss your chance to attend this month’s art talks event and enjoy the fellowship of other like-minded artists – book your tickets via the link below now! Please note this event will be held in the main gallery space which is wheelchair accessible. ### 🎨 [Convergent Bodies: First Leap](https://www.vacantassembly.com/events/convergent-bodies-first-leap)
📆 Sun 20 Sep, 3:00 PM  
📍 Vacant Assembly  
💰 See link  
`art` `exhibition` `research` `ecology`

Image Credit: Bianca Tainsh (microscopy image with Sandra Tuszynska) Convergent Bodies is a visceral self-identification with other interspecies bodies and the emergence of a speculative posthuman future where technology is applied as ecological steward and interlocutor for entanglement with the more-than-human. In an 11-week OCCUPY residency, artist Bianca Tainsh has been visualising this concept through experimental juxtapositions of a vast exploratory archive of organic artefacts and digital documentation, and an accumulating body of aesthetic and conceptual research exploring microscopic and subterranean realms and the relational and metaphysical entanglement of all species. Unlike Haeckel's Art Forms in Nature , Tainsh's archive seeks the wild asymmetries of decay, sexual manifestation, and symbiosis. Convergent Bodies: First Leap is the initial manifestation of a new body of work that will take its cues from Tainsh's residency experiments. #block-f1e6d2ae56c80dd9e160 { } #block-f1e6d2ae56c80dd9e160 .sqs-html-content { } #block-f1e6d2ae56c80dd9e160 { mix-blend-mode: var(--tweak-text-block-blend ); #### [Make-Believe Magic: The Worlds of The Jim Henson Company](https://www.qagoma.qld.gov.au/exhibition/make-believe-magic-the-worlds-of-the-jim-henson-company)
📆 Sat 12 Sep 2026, 10:00 AM – Sun 18 Apr 2027
📍 GOMA, Stanley Place, South Bank
💰 Buy Tickets
`puppetry` `film` `design` `exhibition`

Celebrating 70 years of creativity and innovation, the exclusive-to-Brisbane exhibition ‘Make, Believe, Magic: The Worlds of The Jim Henson Company’ will be an unprecedented exploration of world-building and imaginative storytelling. Founded by Jim and Jane Henson in 1955, The Jim Henson Company has created some of the most cherished characters in screen history and continues to revolutionise the art of puppetry for television and film. Their indelible imprint on entertainment, education and popular culture globally is seen in the much-loved series Fraggle Rock and Fraggle Rock: Back to the Rock, the treasured fantasy worlds of The Dark Crystal and Labyrinth, the iconic characters of the Muppets, and the instantly recognisable residents of Sesame Street. In a world-first, ‘Make, Believe, Magic: The Worlds of The Jim Henson Company’ will include the Great Hall set from the Emmy Award-winning television series Fraggle Rock: Back to the Rock, taking audiences behind the scenes of a working puppeteer’s film set. Immense and immersive, the exhibition will explore the Company’s dynamic trajectory from practical puppetry to today’s animatronic and digital innovations, as well as its ongoing dedication to the craft of creature design and trailblazing developments in on-screen puppeteering. It will feature hundreds of objects, from practical puppets and animatronic creatures to props, costumes, original drawings and moving image clips. Screening alongside the exhibition throughout its run will be three, free film programs at GOMA’s Australian Cinémathèque as well as a suite of QAGOMA’s renowned public, learning and access programs inviting audiences to explore and engage with the exhibition themes and artworks. This exhibition is organised by the Queensland Art Gallery | Gallery of Modern Art in collaboration with The Jim Henson Company. QAGOMA is excited to be celebrating the opening of ‘Make, Believe, Magic: The Worlds of The Jim Henson Company’ in association with Brisbane Festival in September. Works in this exhibition are protected under the Australian Government’s Protection of Cultural Objects on Loan Act 2013. Find out more.

#### [Sorcerers from Bohemia: Picasso’s Saltimbanques](https://www.qagoma.qld.gov.au/exhibition/sorcerers-from-bohemia-picassos-saltimbanques)
📆 Sat 14 Feb 2026, 10:00 AM – Sun 16 Jan 2028
📍 QAG, Stanley Place, South Bank
💰 Free
`picasso` `printmaking` `art` `history` `free`

'Sorcerers from Bohemia' evokes the free-spirited world of the travelling circus performers, or saltimbanques — the subject of Pablo Picasso’s first major body of work in printmaking. 'La Suite des Saltimbanques' is a series of 15 loosely related etchings and drypoints that mark a transitional moment in the young artist’s personal and artistic development. Created on the cusp of Picasso’s Blue (1901–04) and Rose (1904–06) periods, the works are intimately connected with his paintings and drawings of the same era, including the Gallery’s own La Belle Hollandaise of 1905. On moving to Paris in 1904, 22-year-old Picasso encountered a bohemian crowd of acrobats, jugglers and street performers, whom he sketched as they rehearsed and socialised together. His early depictions focus on the vulnerability and poverty of these itinerant figures, whereas later works pay homage to their strong social bonds and the freedom of their creative lives. Appearing individually and in groups, Picasso’s saltimbanques vary widely in age and fulfil a variety of roles, from clown and friend to mother and king. Ushering in a lifelong experimentation with printmaking, 'La Suite des Saltimbanques' offers extraordinary insights into Picasso’s formative artistic concerns and the social milieu he inhabited at the time. In these sensitive and sympathetic portraits, he conveys the heft of a body, the texture of hair, a playful pose or a tender gesture with remarkable economy and elegance of line, ennobling figures who ordinarily existed on the fringes of society.

#### [Towards a Collapsing World: German Expressionism](https://www.qagoma.qld.gov.au/exhibition/towards-a-collapsing-world-german-expressionism)
📆 Sat 14 Feb 2026, 10:00 AM – Sun 16 Jan 2028
📍 QAG, Stanley Place, South Bank
💰 Free
`expressionism` `art` `history` `printmaking` `free`

The early decades of the twentieth century witnessed an extraordinary moment in German art, as artists responded to a modern world in flux with works that engaged deeply with contemporary society, politics and culture. ‘Towards a Collapsing World’ explores this period of significant cultural flourishing through a survey of works on paper by leading figures of German Expressionism. Rather than a single, unified approach, German Expressionism was characterised by a plurality of attitudes and styles, with artist groups such as Die Brücke (The Bridge) and Der Blaue Reiter (The Blue Rider) forming and dissolving in response to fluctuating ideologies. Common to these disparate voices was a shared belief in the power of art to effect social change. The woodcut print became an important vehicle within German Expressionism, reviving a rich medieval heritage. Its stark, simplified forms — expressed through bold, flat patterns and raw aesthetic intensity —conveyed the inner turmoil and social critique of these expressionist artists with impassioned urgency.

#### [Beyond Henson: Puppetry and World Cinema](https://www.qagoma.qld.gov.au/cinema/program/beyond-henson-puppetry-and-world-cinema)
📆 Sat 12 Sep 2026, 12:01 AM – Sun 18 Apr 2027
📍 GOMA, Stanley Place, South Bank
💰 Free
`film` `puppetry` `cinema` `history` `free`

Inspired by the work of The Jim Henson Company and Jim Henson’s Creature Shop, the film program ‘Beyond Henson: Puppetry and World Cinema’ surveys the rich international history of storytelling through the artforms of cinema and puppetry. Presented in conjunction with the ‘Make, Believe, Magic: The Worlds of The Jim Henson Company’ exhibition (12 September 2026 – 18 April 2027), the film program delves into crafted narratives that harness traditions of puppetry and cinema to move beyond belief and into the realms of the imagination. To trace the anatomy of onscreen puppetry is to draw from millennia of performance and craft traditions that extend across the globe. From the marionette puppetry of France and the Bunraku theatre of Japan, to the Wayang Kulit puppetry of Indonesia and the British Punch and Judy show, the democratised nature of the puppet theatre has enabled an unbridled style of performance that can enact the truthful and the taboo. Taking direction from the television series Jim Henson Presents the World of Puppetry 1985, the selected titles include films that precede (and sit adjacent to) productions by The Jim Henson Company, positioning the Company’s work within the broader canon of on-screen puppetry. [sponsorLogos block] QAGOMA acknowledges the generous assistance of the National Film and Sound Archive of Australia, Canberra; the Academy Film Archive, Los Angeles; the British Film Institute, London; the NBCUniversal Archives & Collections, Los Angeles; the Library of Congress, Washington; the National Film Archive, Prague, the Australian Film Television and Radio School, Sydney and the Revival House, Perth in providing materials for this program. Program curated by Victoria Wareham, Australian Cinémathèque with additional film note writing by Rebekah Brammer.

#### [Where the Wild Things Are Made: Jim Henson’s Creature Shop](https://www.qagoma.qld.gov.au/cinema/program/where-the-wild-things-are-made-jim-hensons-creature-shop)
📆 Sat 12 Sep 2026, 12:01 AM – Sun 18 Apr 2027
📍 GOMA, Stanley Place, South Bank
💰 Free
`film` `creatures` `craft` `cinema` `free`

Presented alongside the ‘Make, Believe, Magic: The Worlds of The Jim Henson Company’ special exhibition (12 September 2026 – 18 April 2027), ‘Where the Wild Things Are Made: Jim Henson’s Creature Shop’ celebrates the skill and craftsmanship of the artisans at Jim Henson’s Creature Shop. Highlighting the diverse portfolio of expressive characters and creatures made by the Company for external productions, the selected films showcase the work of Jim Henson’s Creature Shop and how it continues to shape the landscape of cinematic storytelling. An important facet of The Jim Henson Company, Jim Henson’s Creature Shop is internationally renowned for creating pioneering techniques that bring extraordinary characters and stories to life. Formally established following the release of Labyrinth in 1986, Jim Henson’s Creature Shop continues to be an enduring creative hub where performance and craftsmanship is paired with experimentation and ground-breaking technical innovation to transform the imaginary into reality. [sponsorLogos block] QAGOMA acknowledges the generous assistance of the National Film and Sound Archive of Australia, Canberra; the Norwegian Film Institute, Oslo and the Revival House, Perth in providing materials for this program. Program curated by Victoria Wareham, Australian Cinémathèque with additional film note writing by Rebekah Brammer.

📆 Sat 21 Jun 2025, 10:00 AM – Sun 23 May 2027
📍 QAG, Stanley Place, South Bank
💰 Free
`animals` `indigenous` `culture` `art` `free`

Animals have played a central part in Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander culture and spiritual beliefs for over 60,000 years. Native fauna form an integral part of relationships with Country, in community and are a foundation of economies, identity and sustainability. Over many millennia of co-existence animals have been central to survival as a food source, for First Nations people they are also absorbed into lore and culture as images of totemic power; and into the ancestral creation stories that link people, land and animals. ‘Great and small’ is a celebration of these connections.

#### [The God of Small Things: Faith and Popular Culture](https://www.qagoma.qld.gov.au/exhibition/the-god-of-small-things-faith-and-popular-culture)
📆 Sat 20 Sep 2025, 10:00 AM – Mon 5 Oct 2026
📍 QAG, Stanley Place, South Bank
💰 Free
`faith` `culture` `art` `history` `free`

'The God of Small Things: Faith and Popular Culture' draws its title from Arundhati Roy's evocative novel to explore the omnipresence of faith in the mundane and extraordinary alike. Centred around a rare collection of embellished oleographs by Raja Ravi Varma (India, 1848-1906), the exhibition delves into the intersection between devotional imagery and popular culture, capturing the divine as a living part of everyday life. Works from the QAGOMA contemporary Asian collection complement and extend this dialogue, through the juxtaposition of religious and vernacular iconographies with contemporary reflections on belief. QAGOMA has embarked on a landmark conservation and research exchange with colleagues in India to document and conserve a rare collection of Ravi Varma works. Specialists from QAGOMA’s conservation team will collaborate with the Museum of Art and Photography (MAP), Bengaluru, to share expertise and deepen cultural ties. QAGOMA’s conservation and research exchange is supported by the Centre for Australia-India Relations. QAGOMA is proud to be a recipient of a Centre for Australia-India Relations Maitri grant.

#### [Inscribing a Life](https://www.qagoma.qld.gov.au/exhibition/inscribing-a-life)
📆 Sat 27 Sep 2025, 10:00 AM – Sun 18 Oct 2026
📍 GOMA, Stanley Place, South Bank
💰 Free
`art` `history` `memorial` `collection` `free`

‘Inscribing a life’ brings artworks from across the QAGOMA Collection that register existence, histories, and time through the act of mark making. From the brute force recorded in paint on a canvas that expresses strength and vitality, to the time commitment captured in the repetition involved in making intricate forms, the desire to record and share expressions of life is championed in this exhibition. Complementing the adjacent display of ‘Archie Moore: kith and kin’ both presentations share the themes of memorials and cosmic time. Alongside artworks expressing the intensity and wonder of existence are those that celebrate and solemnly honour lives that have come to an end. These artworks speak to the importance of acknowledging loss and how through observing death the public memorial reaffirms the social fabric for the living. The importance of thinking beyond human lifespan can be seen in expressions of the cosmos, 'Inscribing a life' features artworks that grapple with the vast scale and the deep time of the universe.

#### [Geoffrey Burns: Ride the Chronovore](https://www.parkercontemporary.com.au/exhibitions/geoffrey-burns)
📆 Wed 16 Sep, 12:00 PM – Sat 3 Oct
📍 PARKER Contemporary, 38 Hope Street
💰 See link
`contemporary art` `painting` `exhibition` `gallery`

Revisiting locations at different times of year, Geoffrey Burns approaches making as both a method and a measure of time. Works emerge through repeated encounters with place, where observation accumulates and memory takes form through surface and seasonal change. Ride the Chronovore considers time as an active force: consuming, transforming and leaving its evidence behind. Through repetition, Burns traces how surfaces shift, forms appear and recede, and environments register cycles that often unfold beyond human attention. Each return gathers new information while revealing what has altered, disappeared or endured. The fleeting span of a human life meets the slower tempo of the natural world, inviting an attentive and materially grounded way of looking at the quiet force of time. Opening Night Friday September 18 6 - 8pm VIP Preview from 5.30pm Geoffrey Burns in Conversation Sunday September 20 3pm - 4pm + celebratory drinks with the artist until 5pm Artist in Residence Saturday September 26 12pm - 4pm (drop in any time) Morning Musings Workshop Retreat Sunday October 4 10am - 12pm

📆 Wed 23 Sep – Sun 27 Sep
📍 South Bank Parklands, Grey St, South Bank
💰 See link
`dance` `circus` `performance`

A boundary-breaking fusion of human movement and physical structures. DIAVOLO | Architecture in Motion® productions explore innovation and celebrate the complexity of the human condition with an undefinable blend of dance, stunt work, acrobatics, circus arts, and pedestrian movement. The work is athletic, captivating, and visceral. This is a show that will leave you breathless. Visceral and high-octane, ESCAPE blends complex, moving architectural structures with multi-talented performers to create a blood-pumping and emotional multimedia experience. Along with DIAVOLO's signature athletic movement aesthetic, ESCAPE features a collection of the company's most famous architectural structures. Each evokes a story unique to its design, echoing themes of exploration, danger, fear, survival, resilience, and connection, all brought to life through a soundtrack spanning decades and genres, from pop and EDM to rock.

#### [Scott Breton](https://lethbridgegallery.com/artist/)
📆 Fri 25 Sep – Tue 13 Oct
📍 Lethbridge Gallery
💰 See link
`art` `exhibition` `painting` `sculpture` `drawing` `performance`

Brisbane-based figurative artist Scott Breton combines classical training with a uniquely contemporary vision to explore the living presence of figure, form and space in his work. With a practice informed by deep study of traditional drawing, painting and sculptural media, Breton’s compositions often convey a sense of aliveness and psychological depth, where gesture and structure meet imagination and atmosphere. A recipient of Australia’s prestigious A.M.E. Bale Travelling Scholarship, his work bridges the enduring principles of representational art with fresh insights into the human experience. Breton’s upcoming exhibition offers a new chapter in his ongoing investigation of form, psyche and the poetic potential of the painted surface.

#### [Small Figures](https://www.qagoma.qld.gov.au/exhibition/small-figures)
📆 Wed 20 Sep 2023, 10:00 AM – Tue 26 Jan 2027
📍 QAG, Stanley Place, South Bank
💰 Free
`art` `collection` `whimsical` `digital` `free`

The Collection Online team have been busy photographing thousands of artworks as part of QAGOMA’s Digital Transformation Initiative. This makes the Collection available and accessible to people everywhere, in detail never seen before. With dedicated conservators, registrars, technologists and photographers, the project has provided the resources to delve deep into Collection Storage to uncover and care for artworks not displayed in decades. This exhibition represents a small sample of our teams’ favourite finds – the weird and whimsical, the cute and curious. We gratefully acknowledge the hundreds of donors who generously supported the QAGOMA Foundation’s ‘Unlock the Collection’ campaign to digitise the Collection and make artworks more accessible and discoverable online including this diverse range of artworks, from small figures to small frogs. These are some of the artworks we have recently prepared for photography, and just some of the works which made us smile.

#### [Capturing Beauty – Solo show by Carol Oh](https://rqas.com.au/event/carol-oh-2026-show/)
📆 Wed 23 Sep – Sun 27 Sep
📍 Petrie Terrace Gallery, 3/162 Petrie Terrace
💰 See link
`art` `exhibition` `painting`

[vc_row css=”.vc_custom_1523319263370{padding-top: 20px !important;padding-bottom: 30px !important;}”][vc_column][vc_separator color=”custom” border_width=”10″ accent_color=”#4ba686″][vc_column_text]Brisbane-based artist Carol Oh creates works in oil paint and pen that celebrate the timeless beauty of the human form, nature, architecture, and the quiet poetry of everyday life. Her diverse body of work encompasses exquisite floral studies, portraiture, figurative subjects, landscapes, and finely detailed pen drawings of Rome and its enduring architectural splendour. Drawing and painting from a young age, Carol has cultivated a lifelong passion for capturing beauty, atmosphere, and character. Her work reflects a deep appreciation for both the grandeur and subtlety found in the world around us. This exhibition, Capturing Beauty, brings together a collection of portraiture, figurative, floral, and landscape works. Through her art, Carol invites viewers into moments of reflection and connection, creating evocative imagery that lingers in the memory and resonates beyond the canvas. She continues to expand her artistic practice and welcomes commissioned work. Exhibition will be on show at RQAS Brisbane Gallery from Wednesday, 23rd of September to Sunday, 27th of September.[/vc_column_text][vc_separator color=”custom” border_width=”10″ accent_color=”#4ba686″][vc_single_image image=”26108″ img_size=”full”][/vc_column][/vc_row]

💰 From $41.50

⭐ The Pyramids of Egypt have arrived in Brisbane for the first time ! This immersive and groundbreaking expedition takes you back to the golden age of ancient Egypt, allowing you to explore the depths of the iconic pyramids, meet the goddess Bastet, observe the embalming ceremony of Khufu, and much more! Embark on a journey to the past already experienced by more than 1 million people with the Horizon of Khufu! Highlights 📜 The largest immersive expedition of its kind dedicated to ancient Egypt 🧭 Travel back of 4500 years in time to the heart of ancient Egypt 🏆 Over 2.5+ million visitors worldwide 😎 Move freely through the Great Pyramid of Giza in a fully immersive virtual reality experience 🔎 Enter forbidden corridors, uncover the mysteries of the Queen’s Chamber, and meet the goddess Bastet 👨‍👩‍👧‍👧 Enjoy an intimate and educational experience designed especially for small groups 🌟 Developed in collaboration with Egyptologist Peter der Manuelian from Harvard University 🎁 Gift memories to someone special! Purchase a gift card here General Info 📅 Date: from March 2026 ⏳ Duration: approx. 1 hour, including 45 minutes of immersion walking 📍 Location: Fever Immersive Center - Level 1, 123 Gotha St, Fortitude Valley QLD 👤 Age requirement: 10+ with a minimum height of 1.30m (anyone aged 10-15 must be accompanied by an adult; anyone between 15-17 attending without an adult must bring a pre-signed waiver form) ♿ Accessibility: the venue is wheelchair accessible, but electric wheelchairs must switch to manual mode or alternatively use our manual wheelchairs onsite ❓ Please consult the FAQs of this experience here Description Put on your virtual reality headset and fasten your seatbelt! In Horizon of Khufu , travel thousands of years back to the heart of ancient Egypt. Discover the secrets of one of the seven wonders of the world through an innovative concept: the immersive expedition. Explore the Great Pyramid of Giza as it was more than 4500 years ago. Walk through its mysterious corridors, enter the Queen’s Chamber, and even try to meet the goddess Bastet, in a unique experience in Brisbane. Ready to discover how people lived during Egypt’s 4th Dynasty? Get your tickets now for Horizon of Khufu: Journey to Ancient Egypt in Brisbane!

Description Behind the swinging doors, things are not always as they seem: step into Brisbane’s hidden underworld at Moonshine Saloon , where you’ll mingle with locals, play games, and sip on expertly crafted specialty cocktails. Watch out for the Sheriff, though, if they happen to pass through town… one wrong word is all it takes to bring down Cassidy’s empire! What the hell is Trash MTG!? It’s a Magic The Gathering event dual hosted by Netherworld and Vault Games,but you only get to use your junky cheap cards. Commander with all commons in the 99. We want you to MINE YOUR CARDS you’d otherwise ignore. The ones sitting in shoe boxes at the bottom of a cupboard, potentially never to see the light of day again…until now. If you don’t have quite enough cards to put something together, we’ll have a collection of 10,000 cards on site you can dig into to trade in and out of. Pauper Commander follows the normal Commander rules except… your Commander is an uncommon, and the rest of the 99 are commons. Health is a hair lower at 30 and commander damage is 16. In the spirit of ‘Trash MTG’ and an encouragement to use what you have instead of riding the meta wave, we’ll have a house rule that regardless of the history of the card, all common cards included must be common according to the card. If you haven’t played Commander before, the Vault Games and Netherworld crew are happy to teach you and will even have a handful of house decks on hand you can utilise if you don’t have the trash resources to pull a deck together. If you’re after any Magic sealed products, make sure you swing over to Vault Games CBD, Clayfield or online via vaultgames.com.au ! Each ticket comes with your first drink and a custom token card. It happens at 6.30 to 10pm. Tickets to the next Netherworld session can be grabbed via www.netherworldarcade.com/events and Vault Games sessions via www.vaultgames.com.au !

The morning cruise will last for 45 minutes until you reach your next point of call; the Breakfast Creek Hotel. The Breakfast Creek Hotel is a popular hotspot for locals and tourists alike and after a short walk from the landing dock, it is where you can enjoy a delicious meal. Plating up a range of dishes such as Rump steak, Panko Calamari and Caesar Salad, it will be hard to leave the Hotel with a rumbling stomach. 📆 Wed 9 Sep, 10:30 AM – Sat 21 Nov
📍 Cultural Centre Public Pontoon, Cultural Centre Boardwalk
💰 From $60.95
`cruise` `river` `dining` `tour`

Tickets 🎫 Adult ticket for 1 (16-64) Each ticket includes : Lunch All activities Driver/guide Live commentary on board Coffee and/or Tea Snacks Highlights 🛥️ Relax on a river cruise and enjoy a mouth-watering meal on this combo tour 📗 Learn about Brisbane’s history and culture from an informative guide 👓 Admire views of landmarks like Storey Bridge and Kangaroo Point Cliffs General Info 📅 Date: various dates (select when purchasing) 🕒 Time: 10:30am - 12:00pm 📌 Departure point: Cultural Centre Public Pontoon ⏳ Duration: 3 hr 30 mins approx. 👤 Age requirement: 16+ with valid ID ❓ For this event, all sales are final and tickets can’t be refunded, changed or modified. For more information, please refer to our T&Cs Description This cruise to lunch will let you spend the day with two popular Brisbane identities; River City Cruises and the Breakfast Creek Hotel. Aboard the boat, the sights will be accompanied by informative commentary about the region as well as a morning tea to satisfy your on board cravings. The morning cruise will last for 45 minutes until you reach your next point of call; the Breakfast Creek Hotel. The Breakfast Creek Hotel is a popular hotspot for locals and tourists alike and after a short walk from the landing dock, it is where you can enjoy a delicious meal. Plating up a range of dishes such as Rump steak, Panko Calamari and Caesar Salad, it will be hard to leave the Hotel with a rumbling stomach. From here you will once again board the MV Neptune to cruise back to the dock at South Bank within the luscious Parklands.

#### [Vipoo Srivilasa: Express Yourself](https://www.qagoma.qld.gov.au/exhibition/vipoo-srivilasa-express-yourself)
📆 Sat 11 Oct 2025, 10:00 AM – Sun 13 Sep 2026
📍 GOMA, Stanley Place, South Bank
💰 Free
`kids` `art` `emotions` `free`

How do you feel on the first day of school? Or when you win a really hard game? What about when someone you love is upset? Artist Vipoo Srivilasa invites children to explore their emotions through drawing, dancing, and digital activities as part of his Children’s Art Centre exhibition. The project features a reprisal of Vipoo’s 2021 activity ‘Garden of Love’ along with fun new interactives, encouraging visitors to consider the spectrum of human emotion with curiosity and an open mind. About the artist Vipoo Srivilasa is a Melbourne-based, Thai-born artist who primarily makes ceramics. He is also interested in installation and audience participation. Positivity, beauty and accessibility are important attributes of his art practice, and he is particularly known for creating ceramic characters covered in flowers, called ‘flower bears’. Accessibility An exhibition guide in large print and braille is available in the exhibition space. The large print exhibition guide is also available for download as a PDF file: [documentList block]

⭐ Step beyond reality and into another dimension with Brisbane's award-winning Immersive 3D collaborative experience —a mind-blowing adventure that fuses teamwork, technology, and total immersion. Get ready to live the impossible! Highlights 🚀 Dive into the world’s most advanced team-based virtual adventure and experience a total reality transformation 💥 Move freely through your own dedicated space—walk, grab, throw, and high-five your teammates in full 3D immersion 🎮 No motion sickness, no limits—just pure adrenaline and cutting-edge comfort 🌌 Team up to solve epic puzzles, overcome challenges, and celebrate your victories together in real time 🎬 Feel the rush of cinematic-quality visuals and heart-pounding sound that blur the line between the game and reality 👥 Perfect for team building , adult birthdays , kids’ birthdays & hen’s parties . General Info 📅 Date: Select your date directly in the ticket selector 🕒 Time: hourly from 10 a.m. – 10 p.m. ⏳ Duration: 40-50 minutes 📍 Location: Virtual Room Brisbane (Edward St, CBD) 👤 Age requirement: - Time Travel Chapter 1: 8+ (When played with an adult) - Time Travel Chapter 2: 10+ - Press Start: 12+ - Are We Dead?: 14+ - Asterix: Mission Potions!: 8+ (When played with an adult) 📱 For last-minute bookings (within 2 hours of your visit), please call +61 4 2250 0206 👥 Please note: 2-4 players required per game. If you book for more than 4 people, your group will be split into multiple teams playing simultaneously. 📎 You can check the terms and conditions for this event here ❓ You can check the frequently asked questions and answers here 🎁 Give the gift of adventure! Click here to get a gift card 🚀 Time Travel Chapter 1 Alpha team has vanished during their last mission! Slip on your gear and travel through time—from ancient Egypt to the moon landing—to recover lost intel and save humanity before it’s too late. 🌍 Time Travel Chapter 2 The planet is under threat once again. Track down rare energy sources scattered across time—explore Aztec temples, dive into sunken pirate ships, and race against the clock to save Earth! 🕹️ Press Start You thought you were playing the game… until you became part of it! West End Art Club starts in term 2 Can’t wait for school holiday art camp!? You can now join VA resident artist Chloë Waddell every Tuesday after school for some weekly art making. Tuesday afternoon classes will run on a 10 week term schedule with students being introduced to a variety of artmaking materials, skills & techniques across the 10 week class.Classes will be for a maximum of 8 kids to allow for plenty of one on one assistance. A large focus of West End Art Club will be encouraging participants to build their own creative identity and supporting them to realise their own creative ideas while introducing them to new materials, teaching new skills and offering new ways to look at things. Classes at the start of term will be more structured with specific goals and outcomes in mind. As the term progresses and participants build their creative confidence and develop preferences for materials and styles of making, they will have more freedom to explore their own ideas and spend more time using their medium/media of choice. The content of these classes will be flexible to allow us to meet the needs and interests of the children involved. It will include such activities as: drawing, painting, collage, printmaking, sculpture, assemblage etc. If your kids have attended one of Chloë’s workshops before, you can be assured they will be doing new things and exploring new ideas. All Chloë’s workshops are designed with the belief that art is a powerful medium through which children can explore, discover, and express their inner world in a supportive and fun environment. Overall, these workshops lay a solid foundation for your child's creative life and contribute to their holistic development. #### [The Explainer Series: Supermarkets](https://www.slq.qld.gov.au/whats-on/explainer-series-supermarkets)
📆 Thu 24 Sep, 6:30 PM
📍 State Library of Queensland, Stanley Place, South Bank
💰 See link
`history` `technology` `shopping` `culture`

The Explainer Series is about exploring the questions we’re all asking.Supermarkets are part of everyday life, but there’s a lot more happening in the aisles than deciding what’s for dinner. From pricing and loyalty programs to store layouts, online shopping and the growing influence of AI, the way we buy our groceries is changing fast. The Explainer Series: Supermarkets will take a look behind the shelves to explore how Australian supermarkets became the giants they are today - and where they might be heading next. As smaller stores, home delivery, automated fulfillment and personalised shopping reshape the supermarket experience, what do we gain from all that convenience, and what might we be giving up? Join us as we unpack the power and psychology of supermarkets, the technology changing the way we shop, and what the supermarket of the future might look like.

#### [8 Ball Aitken & Gwyn Ashton – ‘SUGAR RUSH’ Launch Tour](https://events.humanitix.com/8-ball-aitken-and-gwyn-ashton-cave-inn)
📆 Fri 18 Sep, 5:00 AM
📍 The Cave Inn, 47 Balaclava St., Woolloongabba, QLD, 4102
💰 AUD 0
`blues` `rock` `guitar` `live music`

8 BALL AITKEN & GWYN ASHTON (UK) are teaming up for a run of gigs to celebrate the launch their new guitar driven, hip-swinging, blues and boogie single SUGAR RUSH featuring Aussie rock icon Ross Wilson (Daddy Cool / Mondo Rock). Catch them at Brisbane's popular live music spot The Cave Inn (Woolloongabba) on Thursday September 17. Doors 6pm, music 7pm. ABOUT GWYN ASHTON (UK) Gwyn Ashton, one of the hardest working and powerful one-man bands, is back touring a new solo album titled Mojosoul and a brand new single Get Outta Town with Guns N’ Roses guitarist Richard Fortus and members of Australian bands Cold Chisel and Superjesus. In 2023 Ashton smashed stages across The Netherlands and Belgium with prog rockers Wishbone Ash, co-headlined the Adelaide Guitar Festival in Australia and relentlessly blazed his way across Wales, England, Serbia, Bosnia, Germany, Czechia, Slovakia and Poland. 2024 dates include an Australian tour and he’s booked for a mainstage performance at the prestigious Great British R&B Festival. Ashton is on the top of the list of the new wave of modern blues rock artists, as spearheaded by The Black Keys, Jack White, Seasick Steve et al. A first class entertainer he oozes stagecraft and showmanship, combining songs and stories from a road well-travelled with dextrous guitar playing, blasting out drum beats on Bill Ward’s old Black Sabbath drum kit, grinding fuzz bass lines with his thumb while he viciously attacks his guitar with a bottleneck slide, singing and blowing blues harp into a lofi radio microphone. Inspired by the likes of Hendrix and David Lindley, his “guit-arsenal” includes a rack full of electric and acoustic instruments including lap slide, resonator, 12-string and old pawn shop guitars. Ashton has had multiple Tracks of The Week in the UK’s prestigious Classic Rock Magazine from 10 of his commercially released albums on which he’s collaborated with Don Airey (Deep Purple), Neil Murray (Whitesnake), Robbie Blunt (Robert Plant) and Rory Gallagher’s band. “The most versatile performer we’ve had” - Deep Ellum Blues Club, Dallas, Texas. “The king of feel” - Robert Plant. “A true bluesman” - Johnny Winter. “A real player” - David Crosby. With these accolades and every recording garnishing numerous Albums of The Week/Month/Year from UK and European guitar and rock press Ashton’s incredible live show keeps growing stronger and he’s bringing it to a town near you soon! Formerly with Virgin Records, France, Gwyn Ashton was lead guitarist in Europe with Band of Friends (Rory Gallagher’s band) and in Australia with Stevie Wright (Easybeats) and Jim Keays (Master’s Apprentices). He has shared stages with BB King, Buddy Guy, Ray Charles, Peter Green, Van Morrison, Hubert Sumlin (Howlin’ Wolf), Mick Taylor, Status Quo, The Yardbirds, Slade, Robin Trower, Steve Hackett, Wishbone Ash, The Troggs and more. He has been invited onstage to jam with Mick Fleetwood, Canned Heat, Walter Trout, Elliot Randall (Steely Dan) and many others. ABOUT 8 BALL AITKEN 8 Ball Aitken has toured twenty countries performing his blues, soul, swamp-rock and slide-guitar. He’s earned a loyal fanbase from his live shows and internet videos, while paying his dues as a guitarist, singer, songwriter and producer. 8 Ball Aitken represented the Blues Association of South East Queensland at the 2026 International Blues Challenge in Memphis, Tennessee, USA with his band ‘8 Ball Aitken & Taya Chani’. The event was held over several nights on Beale Street and attracted 130 acts from around the world. 8 Ball Aitken & Taya Chani made the semifinals. 8 Ball Aitken won second place in the 2021 International New Orleans Cigar Box Guitar Festival. He has also taken home two Queensland Song Awards for blues and country album of the year from his album ‘Rebel With A Cause’. 8 Ball has lived on the Sunshine Coast for the last five years where he runs a recording studio in the Glass House Mountains. 8 Ball regularly tours Australia and USA. He moved to Nashville, Tennessee, USA between the years of 2012 to 2020 and worked with many of the best in the blues music business today. 8 Ball is back with a brand-new collaboration album ‘Nightbird’. He wrote and produced the recording with soul singer Taya Chani. The title-track ‘Nightbird’ immediately hit #1 on the Australian AMRAP Regional Charts in March 2025 for two weeks in a row as did their second single ‘Miss You The Most’ in May. 8 Ball & Taya’s signature harmonies and groovy guitars have proved to be a winning combination making them festival favorites. 8 Ball’s previous two self-produced albums ‘Ice Cream Man’ & ‘Ice Cream Man 2’ both hit #1 on the Australian Blues & Roots Charts. Tour dates, tickets, videos, merchandise and more are all available from 8ballaitken.com [button link="https://events.humanitix.com/8-ball-aitken-and-gwyn-ashton-cave-inn"]Tickets[/button]

📍 47 Balaclava St, Woolloongabba, QLD, Australia, Queensland
💰 AUD 0
`album launch` `country` `rock` `live music`

It's been a hard road but this wagon train has finally pulled up! DEAD HEAD REDEMPTION are proud to launch their debut album THE WENDIGO HUNTERS here at THE CAVE INN on Saturday September 19th 2026. With support from fellow 4017-ers IRESON ROAD and THE COASTAL COWGIRLS, this will be a night of Hickory-smoked entertainment that's sure to tickle the nostalgia buds. NZ brother and tireless publican Adam Christy, of Cardigan Bar and State of Appalachia fame, brings his new project IRESON ROAD to their debut at the Cave. From gritty blues to powerful dark country ballads, we are set for an emotional ride into the sunset, accompanied by wailing guitar and heartbreaking vocals. Playful, talented, and gosh-darn rootin'-tootin', Sandgate's THE COASTAL COWGIRLS are a female-led bayside band that blend the dusty trails of country & Americana with the refreshing breeze of the coast. Delightful originals, river-washed classics, and yee-haw surprises are these boot-stomping blonde's buttercake. Closing the evening, DEAD HEAD REDEMPTION perform their brand-new album The Wendigo Hunters in full from start to finish. Evolved over the past three years, the songs evoke the atmosphere of the mangroves, the dankness of the swamp, and the harshness of the open land. Weaving tales of folklore, injustice, and murder, the songs tramp the trail between blues, outlaw, and pure rock n' roll. Following the closing track, they'll bring the night to a head with more originals, and a few rusty rail reworks for good measure! [button link="https://events.humanitix.com/dead-head-redemption-album-launch"] Tickets[/button]

This September at the Brisbane Convention & Exhibition Centre, QSO under the baton of Jessica Gethin will celebrate Billy Joel in Piano Man. Relax on a luxury catamaran cruise across Moreton Bay through the Islands with a chance to spot dolphins from the viewing deck. Famous for its natural attractions, including a resident population of kangaroos and koalas, the Island also offers walking tracks, marine wildlife viewing, and a relaxed atmosphere. Swim in the Pacific Ocean, take a short guided hike, and have free time to explore. Enjoy your guided visit on this Stradbroke Island Day Trip from Brisbane!

} } ⚖️ Watch drama | 🗳️ Cast a vote | 🕵️ Crack the case ⭐ You've been called for jury duty. Step into a thrilling courtroom drama where the audience is the jury. Listen to testimonies, analyze evidence, and ultimately decide… guilty or not guilty? It's not theatre you watch. It's justice you deliver. The Million Dollar Murder Inspired by true events, this gripping courtroom drama pulls you into a world where greed, passion, and revenge collide — and where the line between victim and villain is razor-thin. The accused stands in the dock, but is this a cold-blooded killer, or a pawn in a far more dangerous game? No DNA evidence. A tangled web of desire and betrayal. And a divorce worth millions. Every witness has a reason to lie. Every motive points somewhere different. Can you untangle the truth before the verdict is cast? 🚩 This experience includes themes of murder and violent death, domestic violence, emotional manipulation. There is a detailed discussion of a homicide. The performance contains moments of intense emotional conflict. Highlights 🤩 Enjoy a live theatre courtroom drama where you decide the final verdict 🫵 Vote during key moments - your decisions shape hidden angles in the case, making every show different! 🕵️‍♂️ Dissect witness testimonies, unpack evidence, and weigh every argument 🎢 A high-stakes case full of twists - more turns than a rollercoaster, and a verdict only you can deliver How it works 1 Take your seat Arrive 30 min early. Doors close at show time — late entry is not permitted. 2 Hear the case Track every testimony, spot the contradictions, and vote at key moments to steer the trial. 3 Deliver your verdict The jury speaks. Guilty or not guilty - the outcome rests entirely with you. Practical info ⏳ Duration 60 minutes Doors open 30 min before 📍 Venue Old Museum Brisbane 480 Gregory Terrace, Bowen Hills, QLD 4006, Australia 👤 Age 12+ Under 16s must be with an adult ♿ Accessibility Wheelchair accessible Wheelchair zone available ✨ Groups & Private Take the stand 30+ people or private show 🎁 Gift card Send gift Gift memories to someone special Frequently asked questions About the experience What is The Jury Experience and what can I expect? The Jury Experience is an immersive courtroom drama where you become part of the story as a juror. Analyze testimonies, cross-examinations, and evidence to uncover the truth - your vote determines the accused's fate. How long does the experience last? The experience lasts approximately 1 hour. Will there be strobe lights? No, there will not be any strobe lights. Are seats numbered? No, seats are not assigned. Seating is first-come, first-served within the zone you select when purchasing your ticket. About your tickets What are the different types of tickets available? A reserve: Best visibility B reserve: Good visibility, located on the sides C reserve: Fair visibility, positioned further back D reserve: Fair visibility, positioned further back behind C reserve Can I buy a ticket on site on the day of the event? Tickets cannot be purchased on site. All tickets must be purchased via the app or website (feverup.com) before arriving at the event. Is it necessary to buy a ticket for my carer? Guests requiring assistance are entitled to one free pass for their personal assistant/support worker. Contact us to receive a voucher for a free carer ticket (based on availability). The regular ticket must be shown together with the carer ticket at entry. Can I change the date of my ticket? Are refunds allowed? Subject to availability, date/time changes are permitted up to 48 hours before the start time. Tickets are non-refundable except as required under Australian Consumer Law. Can I transfer my tickets to another person? Yes, tickets can be transferred to any other Fever account at no additional cost via "Transfer tickets" in the Fever app. Where can I find my ticket? Do I need to print it out? Your ticket is in the "Tickets" section of the Fever app, and also in your purchase confirmation email. You do not need to print it - it's validated via the app on arrival. Access & venue Where does the show take place? The show takes place at The Old Museum, 480 Gregory Terrace, Bowen Hills QLD 4006, Australia. Does the experience take place indoors or outdoors? This experience takes place indoors in a heated/air-conditioned venue. Are food and drinks available on site for purchase? No, food and drinks are not available for purchase on site. Are animals allowed? Animals, except assistance animals, are not allowed. Is the venue accessible? Yes, the event is held in an accessible venue. Accessible seating is limited to Zone A and B only. Are there chairs available? Can I be seated during the experience? Yes, this is a seated experience - seating is available for every guest. Will toilet facilities be available on site? Yes, the venue has toilets; on-site staff can direct you. Is there parking on site? There is no dedicated venue parking, but surrounding parking garages and limited street parking are available. Public transport or sustainable alternatives are recommended. Is cloakroom service available? Unfortunately, this location does not have a cloakroom service. Can I take photos or use my phone during the performance? Photos and videos can only be taken during the curtain call, without flash. Phones must be in silent mode throughout the performance. Can I leave during the performance? What items are prohibited? Out of respect for the performers and the audience, you may not leave your seat except in an emergency. Prohibited items include smoking/vaping, external alcohol, hot food or drinks, strong-smelling food or glass, audio recording devices, drugs, flammables, torches, lasers, professional cameras, promotional items, and selfie sticks/tripods. Seating Map Murder #1 · The Million Dollar Murder

#### [Little Monsters](https://www.netherworldarcade.com/event/little-monsters-6/)
📆 Sun 20 Sep, 9:30 AM
📍 Netherworld, 186 Brunswick Street
💰 $10.00
`kids` `family` `games` `gaming`

Little Monsters is a ticketed event. A ticket must be bought for each adult and child attending. Each $10 ticket comes with a bag of 20 Netherworld tokens to be used during the session. Extra tokens can be purchased as normal. These sessions are suitable for small family groups with children 12 years and under. We’re often asked “Can I bring my tiny terror?” Our underworld policy is that because it can get a little gnarlier as the evening goes on, we don’t allow little ones after 5pm. There’s creatures stuck in the wall that’ll swallow them whole if you’re not careful. To even that out, we host ‘Little Monsters’ from 9.30am to 11.30am – a morning for guardians and monsters to be. The bar will be stocked up with soda, juice and something with less sugar because we’re not all monsters.Please note Netherworld will be closed between 11.30am and Midday. A Little Monsters FAQ Unfortunately during these sessions we cannot cater to large gatherings or parties, as the sessions are more focused on providing a more relaxed space outside of our regular hours for families to enjoy the games together. We don’t do any table bookings during these session – it’s very casual. The session runs from 9.30am to 11.30am, with a 15 minute heads up at 11.15am. We require the venue to be vacated at this time so that we can clean up and refresh the venue for our lunchtime open at midday.No outside food or drink is allowed. If your child has any dietry needs during this time that requires you bring your own food, this is of course perfectly fine. If you have any questions whatsoever, get in touch via info@netherworldarcade.com.

#### [Dinos Alive: An Immersive Experience - Birthday](https://feverup.com/m/636771)
📆 Wed 16 Sep, 9:30 AM – Thu 22 Oct
📍 Fever Skygate | Exhibition and Immersive Experience centre, 30-34 The Circuit, Skygate Home and Life Centre, Brisbane Airport
💰 From $21.90
`art` `science` `history` `family` `kids`

⭐ Dinos Alive is a dinosaur exhibition featuring life-size animated replicas in an immersive Jurassic venue. Walk alongside the massive creatures that roamed our world millions of years ago! Tickets for Birthday Celebrations at Dinos Alive 🎫 Birthday ticket - includes entry to the experience and birthday room for the celebration 🎫 VIP Birthday ticket - includes entry to the experience and birthday room for the celebration, virtual reality and a Dinos Alive poster * Please always select the 'Birthday Room' option when booking so that we can ensure that the room is reserved exclusively for your celebration. ** A minimum of 7 tickets required for a birthday booking. Highlights 🌲 Travel back to the Jurassic in a thematic park full of realistic dinosaurs—see these magnificent creatures in their natural habitat! 🦕 Come face to face with 40+ specimens of moving replicas: T-rex, Stegosaurus, Ankylosaurus, Velociraptors, and many more 🦈 Dive into the depths of the seas and interact with Jurassic sea giants in a unique virtual aquarium 😮 Learn more about the Triassic, Jurassic and Cretaceous periods in a fun and interactive way 👓 Immerse yourself to the fullest with a captivating VR experience (for an additional fee) 👨‍👩‍👧‍👦 Kids can enjoy a “Budding Experts” interactive space, where they can become palaeontologists and tame the most iconic dinosaurs General Info 📅 Dates and times: select your date & time directly in the ticket selector ⏳ Duration: approx. 60-90 minutes. You will have the room booked for you for 30 minutes 📍 Location: Fever Skygate | Exhibition and Experience Centre, 30-34 The Circuit, Skygate, Brisbane Airport 👤 Age requirement: all ages are welcome! ♿ Accessibility: the venue is wheelchair accessible ❓ Please consult the FAQs of this experience here ❓ Please contact us at dinos.brisbane@exhibitionhub.com if you have any questions Birthday Celebrations ⏳ You will have access to the Birthday Room for 30 minutes 🎂 2 soft drinks per guest are included in the birthday package 👥 Please note that any person entering the exhibition or the birthday room needs a valid ticket 👥 If you are unsure about the number of attendees, please select the minimum; you can add more attendees upon arrival ⚠ Tickets are not refundable Description Go back millions of years to a land ruled by giants! This immersive experience in Brisbane brings you face-to-face with the fearsome and spectacular dinosaurs that dominated the animal world for 140 million years. From the turkey-sized Velociraptors to the mighty T-Rex, interact with life-sized, moving animatronics of a large variety of species, and see these magnificent creatures in their natural Jurassic habitat. Step into another world and learn something new in an experience that's fun for the whole family! Get your tickets now to celebrate your birthday with us!
